So recv_first (our top ghost) expects prev's last -> tag LAST=11.\nThus:\n  Irecv(recv_first, prev, tag LAST=11)\n  Irecv(recv_last, next, tag FIRST=10)\n  Isend(send_first, prev, tag FIRST=10)\n  Isend(send_last, next, tag LAST=11)\nMatches. exchangeHalos(const Decomp& d, DeviceMesh& dm,\n                          HaloBuffers& hb, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    if (d.size <= 1 || d.lx == 0 || d.n <= 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const int
